  .status-pending  { border-left: 3px solid #ffc107; }
  .status-active   { border-left: 3px solid #28a745; }
  .badge-pending   { background:#ffc107; color:#000; font-size:.68rem; }
  .badge-active    { background:#28a745; color:#fff; font-size:.68rem; }
  .btn-action      { font-size:.72rem; padding:2px 8px; }
  .table-xs td, .table-xs th { padding:5px 8px; }

  }
  .nav-tabs .nav-link:hover { background:#dee2e6; color:#495057; }
  .nav-tabs .nav-link.active {
    color:#0d6efd !important; background:#fff !important;
    border-color:#dee2e6 #dee2e6 #fff;
    font-weight:600; border-top:2px solid #0d6efd !important;
  }
</style>

<div class="wl-page">

      <small class="text-muted">
        <%= locale==='fr'
          ? 'Consultations en attente et actives'
          : 'Pending and active encounters' %>
      </small>
    </div>
    <form class="d-flex gap-2 search-wrap" method="GET" action="/encounters/worklist">
      <input type="hidden" name="tab" value="<%= filterTab %>">
      <input type="text" name="search" class="form-control form-control-sm"
        placeholder="<%= locale==='fr' ? 'Chercher patient...' : 'Search patient...' %>"
        value="<%= search %>">
      <button class="btn btn-sm btn-primary" type="submit">
        <i class="fas fa-search"></i>
      </button>
      <% if (search) { %>
        <a href="/encounters/worklist?tab=<%= filterTab %>"
          class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-secondary">
          <i class="fas fa-times"></i>
        </a>
      <% } %>
    </form>
  </div>

  <%/* ── Tabs ── */%>
  <ul class="nav nav-tabs mb-0" style="border-bottom:none;">

      <a class="nav-link <%= filterTab==='dept'?'active':'' %>"
        href="/encounters/worklist?tab=dept<%= search ? '&search=' + encodeURIComponent(search) : '' %>">
        <i class="fas fa-building me-1"></i>
        <%= locale==='fr' ? 'Mon departement' : 'My Department' %>
        <span class="badge rounded-pill tab-count ms-1
          <%= filterTab==='dept'?'bg-primary':'bg-secondary' %>">
          <%= deptCount %>

            <%= locale==='fr'
              ? 'Aucun resultat pour "' + search + '"'
              : 'No results for "' + search + '"' %>

            <%= locale==='fr'
              ? 'Aucune consultation en cours.'
              : 'No pending or active encounters.' %>
          <% } %>
        </div>
      <% } else { %>

        <div class="table-responsive">
          <table class="table table-xs table-hover align-middle mb-0">
            <thead class="table-dark">
              <tr>
                <th><%= locale==='fr' ? 'N Dossier' : 'File No.' %></th>
                <th><%= locale==='fr' ? 'Patient' : 'Patient' %></th>
                <th><%= locale==='fr' ? 'Age/Sexe' : 'Age/Sex' %></th>
                <th><%= locale==='fr' ? 'Medecin' : 'Doctor' %></th>
                <th>Dept</th>
                <th>Date</th>
                <th>Statut</th>
                <th style="min-width:220px;">Actions</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              <% encounters.forEach(function(enc) {
                var isPending = enc.encounter_status === 'pending';
                var p = enc.patient;
                var dob = p && p.date_birth ? new Date(p.date_birth) : null;
                var ageYrs = dob
                  ? (new Date().getFullYear() - dob.getFullYear())
                  : null;
              %>
                <tr class="enc-row status-<%= enc.encounter_status %>"
                  id="enc-row-<%= enc.encounter_nr %>">

                        class="text-decoration-none fw-bold" style="font-size:.80rem;">
                        <%= p.name_last %>, <%= p.name_first %>
                      </a>
                    <% } else { %>-<% } %>
                  </td>

                  <td class="text-muted">
                    <%= ageYrs !== null ? ageYrs + (locale==='fr'?' ans':' yrs') : '-' %>
                    <% if (p && p.sex) { %> / <%= p.sex === 'm' ? 'M' : 'F' %><% } %>
                  </td>

                  <td style="font-size:.76rem;"><%= enc.consulting_dr || '-' %></td>

                  <td class="text-muted" style="font-size:.72rem;">
                    #<%= enc.current_dept_nr %>
                    <% if (enc.encounter_class_nr === 2) { %>
                      <span class="badge bg-primary ms-1" style="font-size:.58rem;">
                        <%= locale==='fr' ? 'Hospit.' : 'Inpat.' %>

                      id="status-badge-<%= enc.encounter_nr %>">
                      <%= enc.encounter_status === 'pending'
                        ? (locale==='fr' ? 'En attente' : 'Pending')
                        : (locale==='fr' ? 'Actif' : 'Active') %>
                    </span>
                  </td>

                  <td>
                    <div class="d-flex gap-1 flex-wrap">

                      <% if (isPending) { %>
                        <button class="btn btn-sm btn-success btn-action"
                          onclick="doActivate(<%= enc.encounter_nr %>, this)"
                          title="<%= locale==='fr' ? 'Activer' : 'Activate' %>">
                          <i class="fas fa-play me-1"></i>
                          <%= locale==='fr' ? 'Activer' : 'Activate' %>
                        </button>
                      <% } %>

                      <% if (p) { %>
                        <a href="/patients/<%= p.pid %>/record"
                          class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-primary btn-action"
                          title="<%= locale==='fr' ? 'Dossier' : 'Record' %>">
                          <i class="fas fa-folder-open"></i>
                        </a>
                      <% } %>

                      <% if (!isPending && p) { %>
                        <a href="/prescriptions/encounter/<%= enc.encounter_nr %>"
                          class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-secondary btn-action"
                          title="<%= locale==='fr' ? 'Prescription' : 'Prescribe' %>">
                          <i class="fas fa-prescription-bottle-alt"></i>
                        </a>
                      <% } %>

                      <button class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-warning btn-action"
                        onclick="openTransfer(<%= enc.encounter_nr %>,
                          document.getElementById('dr-<%= enc.encounter_nr %>').value)"
                        title="<%= locale==='fr' ? 'Transferer' : 'Transfer' %>">
                        <i class="fas fa-exchange-alt"></i>
                      </button>
                      <%/* Hidden input to safely pass the dr name to JS */%>
                      <input type="hidden"
                        id="dr-<%= enc.encounter_nr %>"
                        value="<%= (enc.consulting_dr || '').replace(/"/g, '&quot;') %>">

                      <button class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-primary btn-action"

                    </div>

            </tbody>

      <% } %>

          <i class="fas fa-bed me-1"></i>
          <%= locale==='fr' ? 'Hospitaliser' : 'Admit Inpatient' %>
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>

<script>
var _csrf         = '<%= csrfToken %>';
var _currentEncNr = null;
var _confirmActivate = '<%= locale==="fr" ? "Activer cette consultation ?" : "Activate this encounter?" %>';
var _confirmDischarge = '<%= locale==="fr" ? "Cloture et liberer ce patient ?" : "Discharge and close this encounter?" %>';
var _selectDoctor = '<%= locale==="fr" ? "Veuillez selectionner un medecin." : "Please select a doctor." %>';
var _selectWard   = '<%= locale==="fr" ? "Veuillez selectionner un ward." : "Please select a ward." %>';
var _labelActive  = '<%= locale==="fr" ? "Actif" : "Active" %>';
var _labelActivate = '<%= locale==="fr" ? "Activer" : "Activate" %>';

function encodeCSRF(token) {
    return encodeURIComponent(token);
}

async function postAction(url, body) {
    var resp = await fetch(url + '?_csrf=' + encodeCSRF(_csrf), {
        method:  'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body:    JSON.stringify(body),
    });
    return resp.json();
}

function doActivate(encNr, btn) {
    if (!confirm(_confirmActivate)) return;
    btn.disabled = true;
    btn.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-spinner fa-spin"></i>';
    postAction('/encounters/' + encNr + '/activate', {}).then(function(data) {
        if (data.ok) {
            if (data.billWarning) {
                alert('Warning: ' + data.billWarning);
            }
            var badge = document.getElementById('status-badge-' + encNr);
            if (badge) {
                badge.className = 'badge badge-active';
                badge.textContent = _labelActive;
            }
            btn.remove();
        } else {
            alert('Error: ' + (data.error || 'Unknown error'));
            btn.disabled = false;
            btn.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-play me-1"></i>' + _labelActivate;
        }
    });
}

function doDischarge(encNr, btn) {
    if (!confirm(_confirmDischarge)) return;
    btn.disabled = true;
    btn.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-spinner fa-spin"></i>';
    postAction('/encounters/' + encNr + '/discharge', {}).then(function(data) {
        if (data.ok) {
            var row = document.getElementById('enc-row-' + encNr);
            if (row) {
                row.style.opacity = '0.4';
                row.style.transition = 'opacity .4s';
                setTimeout(function() { row.remove(); }, 500);
            }
        } else {
            alert('Error: ' + (data.error || 'Unknown error'));
            btn.disabled = false;
            btn.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-sign-out-alt"></i>';
        }
    });
}

function openTransfer(encNr, currentDr) {
    _currentEncNr = encNr;
    document.getElementById('transfer-current-dr').textContent = currentDr || '-';
    document.getElementById('transfer-staff-select').value = '';
    new bootstrap.Modal(document.getElementById('transferModal')).show();
}

function confirmTransfer() {
    var staffNr = document.getElementById('transfer-staff-select').value;
    if (!staffNr) { alert(_selectDoctor); return; }
    var btn = document.getElementById('btn-confirm-transfer');
    btn.disabled = true;
    postAction('/encounters/' + _currentEncNr + '/transfer', { to_staff_nr: staffNr })
        .then(function(data) {
            btn.disabled = false;
            if (data.ok) {
                bootstrap.Modal.getInstance(document.getElementById('transferModal')).hide();
                location.reload();
            } else {
                alert('Error: ' + (data.error || 'Unknown error'));
            }
        });
}

function openInpatient(encNr) {
    _currentEncNr = encNr;
    document.getElementById('inpatient-ward-select').value = '';
    new bootstrap.Modal(document.getElementById('inpatientModal')).show();
}

function confirmInpatient() {
    var wardNr = document.getElementById('inpatient-ward-select').value;
    if (!wardNr) { alert(_selectWard); return; }
    postAction('/encounters/' + _currentEncNr + '/admit-inpatient', { ward_nr: wardNr })
        .then(function(data) {
            if (data.ok) {
                bootstrap.Modal.getInstance(document.getElementById('inpatientModal')).hide();
                location.reload();
            } else {
                alert('Error: ' + (data.error || 'Unknown error'));
            }
        });
}
</script>

</div><%/* end wl-page */%>
<%- include('../partials/footer') %>

<script>
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
  const typeSelect   = document.getElementById('facility-type');
  const parentField  = document.getElementById('parent-facility-field');
  const parentSelect = parentField.querySelector('select[name="parent_id"]');
  const toggle = () => {
    const isSat = typeSelect.value === 'satellite';
    parentField.style.display = isSat ? 'flex' : 'none';
    isSat ? parentSelect.setAttribute('required','required') : parentSelect.removeAttribute('required');
    if (!isSat) parentSelect.value = '';
  };
  typeSelect.addEventListener('change', toggle);
  toggle();
});
</script>

      <form method="GET" action="/admin/facilities/update" id="selectForm">

            </select>

<script>
document.getElementById('facilitySearch').addEventListener('input', function() {
  const s = this.value.toLowerCase();
  document.querySelectorAll('#facilitySelect option').forEach(o => {
    o.style.display = o.textContent.toLowerCase().includes(s) ? '' : 'none';
  });
});

document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
  const typeSelect  = document.getElementById('facility-type');
  if (!typeSelect) return;
  const parentField = document.getElementById('parent-facility-field');
  const parentSel   = parentField ? parentField.querySelector('select[name="parent_id"]') : null;

  const toggle = () => {
    const isSat = typeSelect.value === 'satellite';
    if (parentField) parentField.style.display = isSat ? 'flex' : 'none';
    if (parentSel) {
      isSat ? parentSel.setAttribute('required','required') : parentSel.removeAttribute('required');
    }
  };
  typeSelect.addEventListener('change', toggle);
  toggle();
});
</script>

          <script>
          (function() {
            var labels  = [<% unpaidLast7.forEach(function(d,i) {
              var p = d.date.split('-'); var lbl = p[1]+'-'+p[2];
            %>'<%= lbl %>'<% if(i<unpaidLast7.length-1){%>,<%} }); %>];
            var sales   = [<% if(typeof salesLast7!=='undefined'){
              salesLast7.forEach(function(d,i){
            %><%= Math.round(d.total) %><%if(i<salesLast7.length-1){%>,<%}})}; %>];
            var unpaid  = [<% unpaidLast7.forEach(function(d,i){
              %><%= d.count %><%if(i<unpaidLast7.length-1){%>,<%}}); %>];
            var ctx = document.getElementById('salesUnpaidChart');
            if (!ctx) return;
            new Chart(ctx, {
              data: {
                labels: labels,
                datasets: [
                  {
                    type: 'bar',
                    label: '<%= locale==="fr"?"Encaisse (FCFA)":"Collected (FCFA)" %>',
                    data:  sales,
                    backgroundColor: 'rgba(13,110,253,.55)',
                    borderColor: '#0d6efd',
                    borderWidth: 1,
                    yAxisID: 'ySales',
                    order: 2,
                  },
                  {
                    type: 'line',
                    label: '<%= locale==="fr"?"Factures payees":"Paid bills" %>',
                    data:  unpaid,
                    borderColor: '#198754',
                    backgroundColor: 'rgba(25,135,84,.12)',

                    pointRadius: 4,
                    pointBackgroundColor: '#198754',
                    fill: false,

                    yAxisID: 'yUnpaid',
                    order: 1,
                  }
                ]
              },
              options: {
                responsive: true,
                maintainAspectRatio: false,
                interaction: { mode: 'index', intersect: false },
                plugins: {
                  legend: { display: false },
                  tooltip: {
                    callbacks: {
                      label: function(ctx) {
                        if (ctx.dataset.yAxisID === 'ySales') {
                          return ' ' + Math.round(ctx.parsed.y).toLocaleString('fr-FR') + ' FCFA';
                        }
                        return ' ' + ctx.parsed.y +
                          ' <%= locale==="fr"?"facture(s) payee(s)":"paid bill(s)" %>';
                      }
                    }
                  }
                },
                scales: {
                  ySales: {
                    type: 'linear', position: 'left',
                    beginAtZero: true,
                    ticks: {
                      font: { size: 10 },
                      callback: function(v) {
                        return v >= 1000

                                else if (nl.includes('discharged') || nl.includes('closed'))  bc = 'bg-secondary';

<script>
  document.querySelectorAll('.dropdown-submenu > a').forEach(function (element) {
    element.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
      e.preventDefault();
      e.stopPropagation();
      let submenu = this.nextElementSibling;
      if (submenu) {
        submenu.classList.toggle('show');
        this.parentNode.classList.toggle('show');
      }
    });
  });
</script>
</body>

<script>
var hasInsurance = false;

function toggleInsurance(checked) {
  hasInsurance = checked;
  document.getElementById('insurance-section').style.display    = checked ? 'block' : 'none';
  document.getElementById('no-insurance-section').style.display = checked ? 'none'  : 'block';
  updatePrice();
}

function setInsuranceName(sel) {
  var opt = sel.options[sel.selectedIndex];
  document.getElementById('insurance_firm_name').value = opt ? (opt.getAttribute('data-name') || '') : '';
  updatePrice();
}

function updatePrice() {
  var sel   = document.getElementById('consultation_id');
  var opt   = sel ? sel.options[sel.selectedIndex] : null;
  if (!opt || !opt.value) {
    document.getElementById('price-display').textContent = '—';
    document.getElementById('computed_price').value = '';
    document.getElementById('patient_share').textContent = '—';
    return;
  }
  var p1 = parseFloat(opt.getAttribute('data-price'))     || 0;
  var p2 = parseFloat(opt.getAttribute('data-price-ins')) || 0;
  var price = hasInsurance ? p2 : p1;

  document.getElementById('price-display').textContent  = price.toLocaleString();
  document.getElementById('computed_price').value       = price;

  if (hasInsurance) {
    var pctEl  = document.getElementById('insurance_pct');
    var pct    = pctEl ? (parseFloat(pctEl.value) || 0) : 0;
    var patAmt = Math.round(price * (1 - pct / 100));
    document.getElementById('patient_share').textContent = patAmt.toLocaleString();
  } else {
    document.getElementById('patient_share').textContent = '—';
  }
}

// Prevent double-submit
document.getElementById('admitForm').addEventListener('submit', function() {
  var btn = document.getElementById('btnAdmit');
  btn.disabled = true;
  btn.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-spinner fa-spin me-2"></i>'
    + ('<%= locale==="fr"?"Traitement...":"Processing..." %>');
});
</script>

<script>
// Photo preview
document.getElementById('photo').addEventListener('change', function() {
  if (this.files[0]) {
    var preview = document.getElementById('photoPreview');
    preview.src = URL.createObjectURL(this.files[0]);
    preview.style.display = 'block';
  }
});

// Review button — validate then show modal
document.getElementById('btnReview').addEventListener('click', function() {
  var nameL = document.getElementById('name_last').value.trim();
  var nameF = document.getElementById('name_first').value.trim();
  var dob   = document.getElementById('date_birth').value;
  var nat   = document.getElementById('citizenship').value;
  var isFr  = '<%= locale %>' === 'fr';

  var missing = [];
  if (!nameL) missing.push(isFr ? 'Nom' : 'Last name');
  if (!nameF) missing.push(isFr ? 'Prénom' : 'First name');
  if (!dob)   missing.push(isFr ? 'Date de naissance' : 'Date of birth');
  if (!nat)   missing.push(isFr ? 'Nationalité' : 'Nationality');

  if (missing.length) {
    alert((isFr ? 'Champs obligatoires : ' : 'Required fields: ') + missing.join(', '));
    return;
  }

  var sex    = document.querySelector('input[name="sex_r"]:checked').value;
  var bg     = document.querySelector('input[name="bg_r"]:checked').value;
  var rh     = document.querySelector('input[name="rh_r"]:checked').value;
  var civil  = document.getElementById('civil_status');
  var rel    = document.getElementById('contact_relation');
  var dobFmt = new Date(dob + 'T00:00:00').toLocaleDateString(isFr ? 'fr-FR' : 'en-GB');

  var rows = [
    [isFr ? 'Nom'             : 'Last Name',      nameL],
    [isFr ? 'Prénom(s)'       : 'First Name',     nameF],
    [isFr ? 'Nom jeune fille' : 'Maiden Name',    document.getElementById('name_maiden').value || '—'],
    [isFr ? 'Date naissance'  : 'Date of Birth',  dobFmt],
    [isFr ? 'Sexe'            : 'Sex',            sex === 'm' ? (isFr ? 'Masculin' : 'Male') : (isFr ? 'Féminin' : 'Female')],
    [isFr ? 'Nationalité'     : 'Nationality',    nat],
    [isFr ? 'Groupe sanguin'  : 'Blood Group',    bg + rh],
    [isFr ? 'État civil'      : 'Civil Status',   civil.options[civil.selectedIndex].text],
    [isFr ? 'Profession'      : 'Profession',     document.getElementById('profession').value || '—'],
    [isFr ? 'Religion'        : 'Religion',       document.getElementById('religion').value || '—'],
    [isFr ? 'Tribu / Origine'  : 'Tribe / Ethnic Origin',
      (function() {
        var el = document.getElementById('ethnic_orig');
        return el && el.selectedIndex > 0 ? el.options[el.selectedIndex].text : '—';
      }())
    ],
    [isFr ? 'Téléphone 1'     : 'Phone 1',        document.getElementById('cellphone_1_nr').value || '—'],
    [isFr ? 'Téléphone 2'     : 'Phone 2',        document.getElementById('cellphone_2_nr').value || '—'],
    [isFr ? 'Email'           : 'Email',          document.getElementById('email').value || '—'],
    [isFr ? 'Adresse'         : 'Address',        document.getElementById('addr_str').value || '—'],
    [isFr ? 'Contact urgence' : 'Emergency',
      document.getElementById('contact_person').value
        ? document.getElementById('contact_person').value
          + (rel.value ? ' (' + rel.options[rel.selectedIndex].text + ')' : '')
          + (document.getElementById('relative_phone').value ? ' — ' + document.getElementById('relative_phone').value : '')
        : '—'
    ],
  ];

  var html = '<div class="row g-0">';
  rows.forEach(function(pair) {
    html += '<div class="col-md-6 d-flex border-bottom py-1 px-2">'
      + '<span class="text-muted me-2" style="min-width:110px;font-size:0.72rem;">' + pair[0] + '</span>'
      + '<strong style="font-size:0.8rem;">' + pair[1] + '</strong></div>';
  });
  html += '</div>';

  document.getElementById('confirmBody').innerHTML = html;
  new bootstrap.Modal(document.getElementById('confirmModal')).show();
});

// Confirm — copy all visible values into hidden inputs, then submit form
document.getElementById('btnConfirm').addEventListener('click', function() {
  var map = {
    'h_name_last':        'name_last',
    'h_name_first':       'name_first',
    'h_name_maiden':      'name_maiden',
    'h_date_birth':       'date_birth',
    'h_nat_id_nr':        'nat_id_nr',
    'h_profession':       'profession',
    'h_religion':         'religion',
    'h_ethnic_orig':      'ethnic_orig',
    'h_email':            'email',
    'h_addr_str':         'addr_str',
    'h_addr_zip':         'addr_zip',
    'h_cellphone_1_nr':   'cellphone_1_nr',
    'h_cellphone_2_nr':   'cellphone_2_nr',
    'h_contact_person':   'contact_person',
    'h_relative_phone':   'relative_phone',
    'h_citizenship':      'citizenship'
  };
  Object.keys(map).forEach(function(hid) {
    var src = document.getElementById(map[hid]);
    if (src) document.getElementById(hid).value = src.value;
  });

  // Select elements
  var civilEl = document.getElementById('civil_status');
  var relEl   = document.getElementById('contact_relation');
  document.getElementById('h_civil_status').value     = civilEl ? civilEl.value : '';
  document.getElementById('h_contact_relation').value = relEl   ? relEl.value   : '';

  // Radio buttons
  var sexChecked = document.querySelector('input[name="sex_r"]:checked');
  var bgChecked  = document.querySelector('input[name="bg_r"]:checked');
  var rhChecked  = document.querySelector('input[name="rh_r"]:checked');
  if (sexChecked) document.getElementById('h_sex').value        = sexChecked.value;
  if (bgChecked)  document.getElementById('h_blood_group').value = bgChecked.value;
  if (rhChecked)  document.getElementById('h_rhesus').value      = rhChecked.value;

  // No phone codes — phone numbers include country code typed directly
  document.getElementById('h_phone_1_code').value = '';
  document.getElementById('h_phone_2_code').value = '';

  document.getElementById('patientForm').submit();
});
</script>

<script>
document.getElementById('patientSearch').addEventListener('input', function() {
  const q = this.value.toLowerCase();
  Array.from(document.getElementById('patientSelect').options).forEach(o => {
    o.style.display = !o.value || o.text.toLowerCase().includes(q) ? '' : 'none';
  });
});
document.getElementById('patientSelect').addEventListener('change', function() {
  document.getElementById('pidInput').value = this.value;
});
</script>

<script>
function loadRelease(encounterNr, patientName) {
  document.getElementById('releaseEncounterNr').value = encounterNr;
  document.getElementById('releasePatientName').textContent = patientName;
  document.getElementById('releaseForm').style.display = 'block';
  document.getElementById('releaseForm').scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
}
</script>

<script>
function exportCSV() {
  const rows = document.querySelectorAll('#patientsTable tr');
  let csv = '';
  rows.forEach(row => {
    const cells = Array.from(row.querySelectorAll('th,td')).slice(0,8);
    csv += cells.map(c => `"${c.textContent.trim().replace(/"/g,'""')}"`).join(',') + '\n';
  });
  const a = document.createElement('a');
  a.href = URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([csv],{type:'text/csv'}));
  a.download = 'patients.csv';
  a.click();
}
</script>

      <%

              var billOpenCount = 0;

                                        <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-start">

                                                <%= p.bill_status==='paid'?'bg-success':

                    <div class="rounded border text-center py-2" style="background:#f8f9fa;">

                        <%= locale==='fr'?'Total Facture':'Total Billed' %>
                      </div>
                      <div class="fw-bold" style="font-size:.95rem;">
                        <%= totBilled.toLocaleString() %> FCFA
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div class="col-4">
                    <div class="rounded border text-center py-2" style="background:#f0fff4;">
                      <div style="font-size:.62rem;color:#6c757d;text-transform:uppercase;">
                        <%= locale==='fr'?'Total Paye':'Total Paid' %>
                      </div>
                      <div class="fw-bold text-success" style="font-size:.95rem;">
                        <%= totPaid.toLocaleString() %> FCFA
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div class="col-4">
                    <div class="rounded border text-center py-2" style="background:#fff5f5;">
                      <div style="font-size:.62rem;color:#6c757d;text-transform:uppercase;">
                        <%= locale==='fr'?'Solde Du':'Balance Due' %>
                      </div>
                      <div class="fw-bold <%= totDue>0?'text-danger':'text-success' %>"
                        style="font-size:.95rem;">
                        <%= totDue.toLocaleString() %> FCFA
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <% if (typeof bills !== 'undefined' && bills.length > 0) { %>
                  <div class="accordion" id="billingAccordion">
                    <% bills.forEach(function(b, bIdx) {
                         var bf     = billFinals.find(function(f){ return f.bill_no == b.bill_no; });
                         var due    = bf ? (bf.amount_due || 0) : 0;
                         var paid   = bf ? (bf.amount_recieved || 0) : 0;
                         var bItems = typeof billItems !== 'undefined'
                           ? billItems.filter(function(i){ return i.bill_no == b.bill_no; })
                           : [];
                         var paidItems = bItems.filter(function(i){ return i.status==='paid'; }).length;
                         var openItems = bItems.filter(function(i){ return i.status==='open'; }).length;
                         var billAccId = 'billAcc-' + b.bill_no;
                         var isPaid    = b.status === 'paid' || b.status === 'closed';
                         var isOpen    = b.status === 'open' || b.status === 'partial';
                         var statusClass = isPaid ? 'bg-success' : due > 0 ? 'bg-danger' : 'bg-warning text-dark';
                         var statusLabel = isPaid
                           ? (locale==='fr'?'Payee':'Paid')
                           : due > 0
                             ? (locale==='fr'?'Impayee':'Unpaid')
                             : (locale==='fr'?'Ouverte':'Open');
                    %>
                      <div class="accordion-item mb-2 border rounded shadow-sm">
                        <h2 class="accordion-header">
                          <button class="accordion-button <%= bIdx===0?'':'collapsed' %> py-2"
                            type="button" data-bs-toggle="collapse"
                            data-bs-target="#<%= billAccId %>"
                            style="font-size:.80rem;font-weight:600;background:#f8f9fa;">
                            <div class="d-flex align-items-center gap-2 w-100 me-2">
                              <i class="fas fa-file-invoice-dollar text-success"
                                style="font-size:.75rem;"></i>
                              <span>
                                <%= locale==='fr'?'Facture':'Bill' %> #<%= b.bill_no %>
                              </span>
                              <span class="text-muted" style="font-size:.70rem;font-weight:400;">
                                &bull; Enc. #<%= b.encounter_nr %>
                              </span>
                              <% if (b.date) { %>
                                <span class="text-muted ms-1" style="font-size:.70rem;font-weight:400;">
                                  <%= new Date(b.date).toLocaleDateString(locale==='fr'?'fr-FR':'en-GB') %>
                                </span>
                              <% } %>
                              <span class="ms-auto d-flex align-items-center gap-1 me-1">
                                <span class="fw-bold" style="font-size:.78rem;">
                                  <%= (b.amount||0).toLocaleString() %> FCFA
                                </span>
                                <span class="badge <%= statusClass %>"
                                  style="font-size:.60rem;"><%= statusLabel %></span>
                                <% if (openItems > 0) { %>
                                  <span class="badge bg-warning text-dark"
                                    style="font-size:.55rem;"><%= openItems %> open</span>
                                <% } %>
                                <% if (paidItems > 0) { %>
                                  <span class="badge bg-success"
                                    style="font-size:.55rem;"><%= paidItems %> paid</span>
                                <% } %>
                              </span>
                            </div>
                          </button>
                        </h2>
                        <div id="<%= billAccId %>"
                          class="accordion-collapse collapse <%= bIdx===0?'show':'' %>">
                          <div class="accordion-body p-3">

                                  <div class="rounded border text-center py-1 px-2"
                                    style="background:#f0fff4;font-size:.72rem;">
                                    <div style="font-size:.60rem;color:#6c757d;text-transform:uppercase;">
                                      <%= locale==='fr'?'Paye':'Paid' %>

                                  <div class="rounded border text-center py-1 px-2"

                                      <%= locale==='fr'?'Du':'Due' %>

                                      <%= (due||0).toLocaleString() %> FCFA
                                    </div>
                                  </div>

                                <% if (bf.patient_amount>0 || bf.insurance_amount>0) { %>
                                  <div class="col-6 col-md-3">
                                    <div class="rounded border text-center py-1 px-2"
                                      style="background:#f0f4ff;font-size:.72rem;">
                                      <div style="font-size:.60rem;color:#6c757d;text-transform:uppercase;">
                                        Ins / Pat
                                      </div>

                                        <%= (bf.insurance_amount||0).toLocaleString() %>

                                      </div>
                                    </div>
                                  </div>

                              </div>

                                <a href="/billing/bill/<%= b.bill_no %>#payment-section"
                                  class="btn btn-sm btn-success"

                                  <%= locale==='fr'?'Encaisser le paiement':'Collect Payment' %>
                                </a>

                                  style="font-size:.76rem;">

                                      <th><%= locale==='fr'?'Article':'Item' %></th>
                                      <th><%= locale==='fr'?'Classe':'Class' %></th>

                                      <th class="text-end">Total</th>

                                            <%= item.article||'—' %>
                                          </strong>
                                          <% if (item.islab) { %>
                                            <span class="badge bg-info text-dark ms-1"
                                              style="font-size:.55rem;">Lab</span>
                                          <% } %>
                                        </td>
                                        <td><%= item.class||'—' %></td>
                                        <td class="text-center"><%= item.units||1 %></td>
                                        <td class="text-end">
                                          <%= (item.unit_cost||0).toLocaleString() %>
                                        </td>

                                          <%= (item.amount||0).toLocaleString() %> FCFA
                                        </td>
                                        <td>

                                            <%= item.status==='paid'?'bg-success':
                                                item.status==='open'?'bg-warning text-dark':

                                              style="font-size:.55rem;background:#6f42c1;">
                                              <i class="fas fa-check me-1"></i>Del.

                                  </tbody>
                                  <tfoot class="table-light">

                                      <td colspan="4" class="text-end fw-bold"
                                        style="font-size:.76rem;">Total</td>
                                      <td class="text-end fw-bold text-primary">
                                        <%= bItems.reduce(function(s,i){
                                          return s+(i.amount||0);},0).toLocaleString() %> FCFA
                                      </td>
                                      <td></td>
                                    </tr>
                                  </tfoot>
                                </table>
                              </div>
                            <% } else { %>
                              <div class="text-center text-muted py-3" style="font-size:.78rem;">
                                <i class="fas fa-receipt me-1"></i>
                                <%= locale==='fr'?'Aucun article sur cette facture.':'No items for this bill.' %>
                              </div>
                            <% } %>

                              <%= (bf.amount_due||0).toLocaleString() %> FCFA</td>

                              <%= bf.status==='closed'?'bg-success':bf.amount_due>0?'bg-danger':'bg-secondary' %>"

<script>
// ── Prescription split pane
function showPrx(encNr, el) {
  document.querySelectorAll('#prx-list .list-item').forEach(function(li){li.classList.remove('active');});
  el.classList.add('active');
  document.querySelectorAll('#prx-detail > div').forEach(function(p){p.style.display='none';});
  var panel = document.getElementById('prx-panel-' + encNr);
  if (panel) panel.style.display = 'block';
}

// ── Record bills tab: load items via JSON when bill clicked ──────
var _recBillLoaded = {};

function loadBillItems(billNo, el) {
  // Highlight selected bill
  document.querySelectorAll('#rec-bill-list .list-item').forEach(function(li) {
    li.classList.remove('active');
  });
  if (el) el.classList.add('active');

  var placeholder = document.getElementById('rec-bill-placeholder');
  var content     = document.getElementById('rec-bill-content');
  if (placeholder) placeholder.style.display = 'none';
  if (content) {
    content.style.display = '';
    content.innerHTML = '<div class="text-center py-4"><i class="fas fa-spinner fa-spin text-muted"></i></div>';
  }

  // Use cached result if already loaded
  if (_recBillLoaded[billNo]) {
    renderBillItems(billNo, _recBillLoaded[billNo]);
    return;
  }

  fetch('/billing/bill/' + billNo + '/items-json')
    .then(function(r) { return r.json(); })
    .then(function(data) {
      if (data.ok) {
        _recBillLoaded[billNo] = data;
        renderBillItems(billNo, data);
      } else {
        if (content) content.innerHTML = '<div class="text-danger p-3">Error: ' + (data.error||'Failed') + '</div>';
      }
    })
    .catch(function(err) {
      if (content) content.innerHTML = '<div class="text-danger p-3">Network error: ' + err.message + '</div>';
    });
}

function renderBillItems(billNo, data) {
  var content = document.getElementById('rec-bill-content');
  if (!content) return;

  var bill    = data.bill;
  var items   = data.items;
  var canPay  = <%= (typeof hasAnyPermission !== 'undefined' && hasAnyPermission(['Billing.Collect.Payment','Billing.Create.Invoice'])) ? 'true' : 'false' %>;
  var locale  = '<%= locale %>';
  var isFr    = locale === 'fr';

  var totalAmt = items.reduce(function(s,i){ return s + (i.amount||0); }, 0);
  var paidAmt  = items.filter(function(i){ return i.status==='paid'; })
                      .reduce(function(s,i){ return s + (i.amount||0); }, 0);
  var openAmt  = totalAmt - paidAmt;

  // Header
  var sClass = bill.status==='paid'   ? 'bg-success' :
               bill.status==='partial' ? 'bg-primary'  : 'bg-warning text-dark';
  var sLabel = bill.status==='paid'
    ? (isFr?'Payee':'Paid')
    : bill.status==='partial'
      ? (isFr?'Partielle':'Partial')
      : (isFr?'Ouverte':'Open');

  var payBtn = (bill.status !== 'paid' && canPay)
    ? '<a href="/billing/bill/' + billNo + '#payment-section" class="btn btn-sm btn-success" style="font-size:.70rem;padding:2px 8px;">' +
      '<i class="fas fa-cash-register me-1"></i>' + (isFr?'Payer':'Pay') + '</a>'
    : '';

  var html = '<div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center mb-2">' +
    '<span class="fw-bold" style="font-size:.80rem;">' +
    (isFr?'Facture':'Bill') + ' #' + billNo +
    ' <span class="badge ' + sClass + ' ms-1" style="font-size:.60rem;">' + sLabel + '</span>' +
    '</span>' + payBtn + '</div>';

  if (items.length === 0) {
    html += '<div class="text-muted text-center py-3" style="font-size:.78rem;">' +
            (isFr?'Aucun article.':'No items.') + '</div>';
  } else {
    html += '<table class="table table-xs align-middle mb-2"><thead class="table-light"><tr>' +
            '<th>' + (isFr?'Article':'Item') + '</th>' +
            '<th class="text-center">' + (isFr?'Qte':'Qty') + '</th>' +
            '<th class="text-end">Total</th><th>Statut</th></tr></thead><tbody>';

    items.forEach(function(item) {
      var isPaid   = item.status === 'paid';
      var rowStyle = isPaid ? 'opacity:.65;' : '';
      var badgeCls = isPaid ? 'bg-success' : 'bg-warning text-dark';
      var badgeLbl = isPaid ? (isFr?'Paye':'Paid') : (isFr?'Ouvert':'Open');
      html += '<tr style="' + rowStyle + '">' +
        '<td><div style="font-weight:600;font-size:.78rem;">' + item.article + '</div>' +
        (item.class ? '<div style="font-size:.63rem;color:#adb5bd;">' + item.class + '</div>' : '') +
        '</td>' +
        '<td class="text-center">' + item.units + '</td>' +
        '<td class="text-end fw-bold" style="font-size:.78rem;">' +
          Math.round(item.amount||0).toLocaleString('fr-FR') + ' FCFA</td>' +
        '<td><span class="badge ' + badgeCls + '" style="font-size:.60rem;">' + badgeLbl + '</span></td>' +
        '</tr>';
    });

    html += '</tbody><tfoot class="table-light">' +
      '<tr><td colspan="2" class="text-end" style="font-size:.76rem;">Total</td>' +
      '<td class="text-end fw-bold" style="font-size:.78rem;">' +
        Math.round(totalAmt).toLocaleString('fr-FR') + ' FCFA</td><td></td></tr>';

    if (paidAmt > 0) {
      html += '<tr><td colspan="2" class="text-end text-success" style="font-size:.72rem;">' +
              (isFr?'Paye':'Paid') + '</td>' +
              '<td class="text-end text-success fw-bold" style="font-size:.72rem;">' +
              Math.round(paidAmt).toLocaleString('fr-FR') + ' FCFA</td><td></td></tr>';
      html += '<tr><td colspan="2" class="text-end text-danger" style="font-size:.72rem;">' +
              (isFr?'Reste':'Balance') + '</td>' +
              '<td class="text-end text-danger fw-bold" style="font-size:.72rem;">' +
              Math.round(openAmt).toLocaleString('fr-FR') + ' FCFA</td><td></td></tr>';
    }
    html += '</tfoot></table>';
  }

  content.innerHTML = html;
}

// Auto-load the first bill when tab is clicked
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {

  // Activate tab from ?tab= query param (e.g. after prescription submit)
  var urlParams = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
  var tabParam  = urlParams.get('tab');
  if (tabParam) {
    var tabLink = document.querySelector('[href="#tab-' + tabParam + '"]');
    if (tabLink && typeof bootstrap !== 'undefined') {
      var bsTab = new bootstrap.Tab(tabLink);
      bsTab.show();
    }
  }

  // When Billing tab opens: auto-select first bill
  var billingTabEl = document.querySelector('[href="#tab-billing"]');
  if (billingTabEl) {
    billingTabEl.addEventListener('shown.bs.tab', function() {
      var firstItem = document.querySelector('#bill-list .list-item');
      if (firstItem) {
        var billNo = parseInt(firstItem.id.replace('bill-li-',''));
        if (billNo) showBill(billNo, firstItem);
      }
    });
  }
});

// ── CSRF token for fetch ──────────────────────────────────────────
var _recCsrf = '<%= csrfToken %>';

// ── VITALS ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function toggleVitalsForm(encNr) {
  var form = document.getElementById('vitals-form-' + encNr);
  if (!form) return;
  var visible = form.style.display !== 'none';
  form.style.display = visible ? 'none' : '';
  if (!visible) {
    var valEl = document.getElementById('vval-' + encNr);
    if (valEl) valEl.focus();
  }
}
function saveVital(encNr) {
  var typeEl = document.getElementById('vtype-' + encNr);
  var valEl  = document.getElementById('vval-' + encNr);
  var type   = typeEl ? typeEl.value : '';
  var val    = valEl  ? valEl.value.trim() : '';
  if (!val) {
    alert('<%= locale==="fr"?"Veuillez saisir une valeur.":"Please enter a value." %>');
    if (valEl) valEl.focus();
    return;
  }
  var btn = null;
  fetch('/encounters/' + encNr + '/vitals?_csrf=' + encodeURIComponent(_recCsrf), {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify({ msr_type_nr: type, value: val }),
  })
  .then(function(r) { return r.json(); })
  .then(function(data) {
    if (data.ok) {
      toggleVitalsForm(encNr);
      if (valEl) valEl.value = '';
      window.location.reload();
    } else {
      alert('Error: ' + (data.error || 'Unknown'));
    }
  });
}

// ── NOTES ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function saveNote(encNr) {
  var ta = document.getElementById('note-text-' + encNr);
  var text = ta ? ta.value.trim() : '';
  if (!text) { alert('<%= locale==="fr"?"Veuillez saisir une note.":"Please enter a note." %>'); return; }

  fetch('/encounters/' + encNr + '/notes?_csrf=' + encodeURIComponent(_recCsrf), {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify({ notes: text }),
  })
  .then(function(r) { return r.json(); })
  .then(function(data) {
    if (data.ok) {
      if (ta) ta.value = '';
      window.location.reload();
    } else {
      alert('Error: ' + (data.error || 'Unknown'));
    }
  });
}

// ── DIAGNOSES ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
var _dxCarts   = {};  // { encNr: [ {code, label, type} ] }
var _dxTimers  = {};

function dxSearch(encNr, q) {
  clearTimeout(_dxTimers[encNr]);
  var resultsEl = document.getElementById('dx-results-' + encNr);
  if (!q || q.length < 2) {
    if (resultsEl) resultsEl.style.display = 'none';
    return;
  }
  _dxTimers[encNr] = setTimeout(function() {
    fetch('/encounters/cim10/search?q=' + encodeURIComponent(q))
      .then(function(r) { return r.json(); })
      .then(function(data) {
        if (!resultsEl) return;
        if (!data.ok || data.results.length === 0) {
          resultsEl.innerHTML = '<div class="dx-result-item text-muted"><%= locale==="fr"?"Aucun resultat.":"No results." %></div>';
          resultsEl.style.display = '';
          return;
        }
        var html = '';
        data.results.forEach(function(r) {
          html += '<div class="dx-result-item" onclick="addDxToCart(' + encNr + ',' +
            JSON.stringify(r).replace(/"/g,'&quot;') + ')">' +
            '<code style="color:#dc3545;font-size:.72rem;">' + r.code + '</code>' +
            ' <span>' + r.label + '</span>' +
            '</div>';
        });
        resultsEl.innerHTML = html;
        resultsEl.style.display = '';
      });
  }, 300);
}

function addDxToCart(encNr, item) {
  if (!_dxCarts[encNr]) _dxCarts[encNr] = [];
  var already = _dxCarts[encNr].some(function(d){ return d.code === item.code; });
  if (already) return;
  _dxCarts[encNr].push({ code: item.code, label: item.label, type: 'main' });
  renderDxCart(encNr);
  // Clear search
  var inp = document.getElementById('dx-search-' + encNr);
  var res = document.getElementById('dx-results-' + encNr);
  if (inp) inp.value = '';
  if (res) res.style.display = 'none';
}

function removeDxFromCart(encNr, code) {
  if (!_dxCarts[encNr]) return;
  _dxCarts[encNr] = _dxCarts[encNr].filter(function(d){ return d.code !== code; });
  renderDxCart(encNr);
}

function renderDxCart(encNr) {
  var cartEl   = document.getElementById('dx-cart-' + encNr);
  var saveWrap = document.getElementById('dx-save-wrap-' + encNr);
  if (!cartEl) return;
  var cart = _dxCarts[encNr] || [];
  if (cart.length === 0) {
    cartEl.innerHTML = '';
    if (saveWrap) saveWrap.style.display = 'none';
    return;
  }
  if (saveWrap) saveWrap.style.display = '';
  var html = '';
  cart.forEach(function(d) {
    html += '<span class="dx-cart-tag">' +
      '<code style="color:#dc3545;">' + d.code + '</code>' +
      ' ' + d.label +
      '<button onclick="removeDxFromCart(' + encNr + ','' + d.code + '')" ' +
      'style="background:none;border:none;padding:0;cursor:pointer;font-size:.7rem;color:#6c757d;">' +
      '<i class="fas fa-times"></i></button>' +
      '</span>';
  });
  cartEl.innerHTML = html;
}

function saveDiagnoses(encNr) {
  var cart = _dxCarts[encNr] || [];
  if (cart.length === 0) return;

  fetch('/encounters/' + encNr + '/diagnoses?_csrf=' + encodeURIComponent(_recCsrf), {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify({ diagnoses: cart }),
  })
  .then(function(r) { return r.json(); })
  .then(function(data) {
    if (data.ok) {
      _dxCarts[encNr] = [];
      window.location.reload();
    } else {
      alert('Error: ' + (data.error || 'Unknown'));
    }
  });
}

function deleteDx(dxNr, btn) {
  if (!confirm('<%= locale==="fr"?"Supprimer ce diagnostic ?":"Remove this diagnosis?" %>')) return;
  fetch('/encounters/diagnoses/' + dxNr + '?_csrf=' + encodeURIComponent(_recCsrf), {
    method: 'DELETE',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
  })
  .then(function(r) { return r.json(); })
  .then(function(data) {
    if (data.ok) {
      var badge = document.getElementById('dx-badge-' + dxNr);
      if (badge) badge.remove();
    } else {
      alert('Error: ' + (data.error || 'Unknown'));
    }
  });
}

// Close dx dropdown on outside click
document.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
  if (!e.target.closest('.dx-search-wrap')) {
    document.querySelectorAll('.dx-results').forEach(function(el) {
      el.style.display = 'none';
    });
  }
});

// ── Billing split pane
// showBill removed — accordion handles billing display

// ── Past encounter expand/collapse
function togglePast(i) {
  var det = document.getElementById('past-det-' + i);
  var ico = document.getElementById('past-ico-' + i);
  var hdr = document.getElementById('past-hdr-' + i);
  if (det.style.display === 'block') {
    det.style.display = 'none';
    ico.className = 'fas fa-chevron-right text-muted';
    ico.style.fontSize = '.6rem';
    hdr.parentElement.classList.remove('open');
  } else {
    det.style.display = 'block';
    ico.className = 'fas fa-chevron-down text-primary';
    ico.style.fontSize = '.6rem';
    hdr.parentElement.classList.add('open');
  }
}

function toggleAllPast() {
  document.querySelectorAll('[id^="past-det-"]').forEach(function(d){d.style.display='none';});
  document.querySelectorAll('[id^="past-ico-"]').forEach(function(i){
    i.className = 'fas fa-chevron-right text-muted'; i.style.fontSize='.6rem';
  });
}
</script>

                      <div style="font-size:.68rem;color:#adb5bd;" class="mt-1">
                        <%= p.price ? p.price + ' FCFA' : '' %>
                        <% if (p.bill_number) { %>
                          &bull; Bill #<%= p.bill_number %>
                          <span class="badge <%= p.bill_status==='paid'?'bg-success':p.bill_status==='open'?'bg-warning text-dark':'bg-secondary' %>"
                            style="font-size:.55rem;"><%= p.bill_status %></span>
                        <% } %>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    <div class="ms-2 text-end">
                      <span class="badge bg-success" style="font-size:.60rem;">Active</span>
                      <br>
                      <button class="btn btn-xs btn-outline-danger mt-1"
                        style="font-size:.65rem;padding:1px 5px;"
                        onclick="stopPrx(<%= p.nr %>, this)"
                        title="<%= locale==='fr'?'Arreter':'Stop' %>">
                        <i class="fas fa-stop me-1"></i>
                        <%= locale==='fr'?'Arreter':'Stop' %>
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              <% }); %>
            </div>
          <% } %>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>

  </div>
</div>
</div>

<script>
var _csrf    = '<%= csrfToken %>';
var _encNr   = <%= encounter.encounter_nr %>;
var _locale  = '<%= locale %>';
var _isFr    = _locale === 'fr';
var _cart    = [];
var _selItem = null;
var _searchTimer = null;

// ── Item search ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
document.getElementById('item-search').addEventListener('input', function() {
    clearTimeout(_searchTimer);
    var q = this.value.trim();
    if (q.length < 2) {
        document.getElementById('search-results').style.display = 'none';
        return;
    }
    _searchTimer = setTimeout(function() { doSearch(q); }, 300);
});

document.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
    if (!e.target.closest('.search-wrap')) {
        document.getElementById('search-results').style.display = 'none';
    }
});

function doSearch(q) {
    fetch('/prescriptions/search?q=' + encodeURIComponent(q))
        .then(function(r) { return r.json(); })
        .then(function(data) {
            var box = document.getElementById('search-results');
            if (!data.ok || data.items.length === 0) {
                box.innerHTML = '<div class="search-result text-muted">' +
                    (_isFr ? 'Aucun resultat.' : 'No results.') + '</div>';
                box.style.display = '';
                return;
            }
            var html = '';
            data.items.forEach(function(item) {
                html += '<div class="search-result" onclick="selectItem(' +
                    JSON.stringify(item).replace(/"/g,'&quot;') + ')">' +
                    '<div class="fw-bold">' + item.description + '</div>' +
                    '<div style="font-size:.68rem;color:#6c757d;">' +
                    item.item_number + ' &bull; ' + item.drug_class +
                    ' &bull; <strong>' + Math.round(item.price).toLocaleString('fr-FR') + ' FCFA</strong>' +
                    '</div></div>';
            });
            box.innerHTML = html;
            box.style.display = '';
        });
}

function selectItem(item) {
    _selItem = item;
    document.getElementById('search-results').style.display = 'none';
    document.getElementById('item-search').value = item.description;
    document.getElementById('selected-item-name').textContent = item.description;
    document.getElementById('selected-item-price').textContent =
        Math.round(item.price).toLocaleString('fr-FR') + ' FCFA';
    document.getElementById('inp-units').value  = 1;
    document.getElementById('inp-dosage').value = '';
    document.getElementById('inp-notes').value  = '';
    document.getElementById('selected-item-form').style.display = '';
    document.getElementById('inp-dosage').focus();
}

function clearItemForm() {
    _selItem = null;
    document.getElementById('item-search').value = '';
    document.getElementById('selected-item-form').style.display = 'none';
}

// ── Cart ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function addToCart() {
    if (!_selItem) return;
    var units   = Math.max(1, parseInt(document.getElementById('inp-units').value) || 1);
    var dosage  = document.getElementById('inp-dosage').value.trim();
    var apptype = document.getElementById('inp-apptype').value;
    var notes   = document.getElementById('inp-notes').value.trim();

    if (!dosage) {
        alert(_isFr ? 'Veuillez saisir la posologie.' : 'Please enter the dosage.');
        document.getElementById('inp-dosage').focus();
        return;
    }
    if (!notes) {
        alert(_isFr ? 'Veuillez saisir les instructions.' : 'Please enter the instructions/notes.');
        document.getElementById('inp-notes').focus();
        return;
    }

    var total = Math.round(_selItem.price * units);
    _cart.push({
        item_id:            _selItem.item_id,
        description:        _selItem.description,
        drug_class:         _selItem.drug_class,
        price:              _selItem.price,
        units:              units,
        dosage:             dosage,
        application_type_nr: apptype,
        notes:              notes,
        total:              total,
    });

    clearItemForm();
    renderCart();
}

function removeFromCart(idx) {
    _cart.splice(idx, 1);
    renderCart();
}

function renderCart() {
    var count = _cart.length;
    document.getElementById('cart-count').textContent = count;

    var grandTotal = _cart.reduce(function(s, i) { return s + i.total; }, 0);
    document.getElementById('cart-total').textContent =
        Math.round(grandTotal).toLocaleString('fr-FR') + ' FCFA';

    var empty   = document.getElementById('cart-empty');
    var items   = document.getElementById('cart-items');
    var footer  = document.getElementById('cart-footer');

    if (count === 0) {
        empty.style.display  = '';
        items.innerHTML      = '';
        footer.style.display = 'none';
        return;
    }
    empty.style.display  = 'none';
    footer.style.display = '';

    var html = '';
    _cart.forEach(function(item, idx) {
        html += '<div class="cart-row px-3">' +
            '<div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-start">' +
            '<div style="flex:1;">' +
            '<div class="fw-bold" style="font-size:.80rem;">' + item.description + '</div>' +
            '<div class="text-muted" style="font-size:.70rem;">' +
            (_isFr?'Qte:':'Qty:') + ' ' + item.units +
            ' &bull; ' + (_isFr?'Posologie:':'Dosage:') + ' ' + item.dosage +
            ' &bull; ' + item.notes + '</div>' +
            '</div>' +
            '<div class="ms-2 text-end">' +
            '<div class="fw-bold text-primary" style="font-size:.80rem;">' +
            Math.round(item.total).toLocaleString('fr-FR') + ' FCFA</div>' +
            '<button class="btn btn-xs btn-outline-danger mt-1" style="font-size:.62rem;padding:1px 5px;"' +
            ' onclick="removeFromCart(' + idx + ')">' +
            '<i class="fas fa-times"></i></button>' +
            '</div></div></div>';
    });
    items.innerHTML = html;
}

// ── Submit prescription ───────────────────────────────────────────
function submitPrescription() {
    if (_cart.length === 0) return;
    var btn = document.getElementById('btn-submit-prx');
    btn.disabled = true;
    btn.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-spinner fa-spin me-2"></i>' +
        (_isFr ? 'Traitement...' : 'Processing...');

    var payload = { items: _cart.map(function(c) {
        return {
            item_id:            c.item_id,
            dosage:             c.dosage,
            application_type_nr: c.application_type_nr,
            notes:              c.notes,
            units:              c.units,
        };
    })};

    fetch('/prescriptions/encounter/' + _encNr + '?_csrf=' + encodeURIComponent(_csrf), {
        method:  'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body:    JSON.stringify(payload),
    })
    .then(function(r) { return r.json(); })
    .then(function(data) {
        if (data.ok) {
            window.location.href = data.redirect;
        } else {
            alert((_isFr ? 'Erreur: ' : 'Error: ') + (data.error || 'Unknown error'));
            btn.disabled = false;
            btn.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-check me-2"></i>' +
                (_isFr ? 'Valider la prescription' : 'Submit Prescription');
        }
    })
    .catch(function(err) {
        alert('Network error: ' + err.message);
        btn.disabled = false;
        btn.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-check me-2"></i>' +
            (_isFr ? 'Valider la prescription' : 'Submit Prescription');
    });
}

// ── Stop prescription ─────────────────────────────────────────────
function stopPrx(prxNr, btn) {
    if (!confirm(_isFr ? 'Arreter cette prescription ?' : 'Stop this prescription?')) return;
    btn.disabled = true;

    fetch('/prescriptions/' + prxNr + '/stop?_csrf=' + encodeURIComponent(_csrf), {
        method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    })
    .then(function(r) { return r.json(); })
    .then(function(data) {
        if (data.ok) {
            var row = document.getElementById('prx-row-' + prxNr);
            if (row) {
                row.style.opacity = '0.4';
                row.style.textDecoration = 'line-through';
                btn.remove();
            }
        } else {
            alert('Error: ' + (data.error || 'Unknown'));
            btn.disabled = false;
        }
    });
}
</script>

<%- include('../partials/footer') %>

<script>
  function debounce(func, delay) {
    let timeout;
    return function(...args) { clearTimeout(timeout); timeout = setTimeout(() => func.apply(this, args), delay); };
  }

  document.getElementById('personSearch').addEventListener('input', debounce(function () {
    const q = this.value;
    const sel = document.getElementById('person_id');
    while (sel.options.length > 1) sel.remove(1);
    if (q.length < 3) return;
    fetch(`/admin/users/personnel-search?query=${encodeURIComponent(q)}`)
      .then(r => r.json())
      .then(data => data.forEach(p => {
        const opt = document.createElement('option');
        opt.value = p.pid;
        opt.textContent = `${p.name_first || ''} ${p.name_last || ''} (PID: ${p.pid})`;
        opt.dataset.firstname = p.name_first || '';
        opt.dataset.lastname  = p.name_last  || '';
        opt.dataset.email     = p.email      || '';
        sel.appendChild(opt);
      }));
  }, 300));

  document.getElementById('person_id').addEventListener('change', function () {
    const opt = this.options[this.selectedIndex];
    if (opt.value) {
      const last  = opt.dataset.lastname;
      const first = opt.dataset.firstname;
      let uname = last.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, '');
      if (first.length >= 2) uname += first.substring(0, 2).toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, '');
      document.getElementById('username').value = uname;
      document.getElementById('email').value    = opt.dataset.email;
    } else {
      document.getElementById('username').value = '';
      document.getElementById('email').value    = '';
    }
  });
</script>

              <% }) %>
            </select>

            <label class="form-label"><%= t.users.facility %></label>
            <select name="facility_id" class="form-select form-select-sm" required>
              <option value=""><%= t.users.selectFacility %></option>

            <label class="form-label"><%= t.users.department %></label>
            <select name="dept_nr" class="form-select form-select-sm">
              <option value=""><%= t.users.selectDept %></option>
              <% departments.forEach(dept => { %>
                <option value="<%= dept.nr %>" <%= userToEdit.dept_nr === dept.nr ? 'selected' : '' %>><%= dept.name_formal %></option>
              <% }) %>
            </select>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div class="text-end mt-3">
          <a href="/" class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-secondary me-1"><%= t.common.cancel %></a>
          <button type="submit" class="btn btn-sm btn-success">
            <i class="fas fa-save me-1"></i><%= t.users.update %>
          </button>
        </div>
      </form>
      <% } %>

    </div>
  </div>
</div>

<script>
function debounce(fn, ms) {
  let t; return function(...a) { clearTimeout(t); t = setTimeout(() => fn.apply(this,a), ms); };
}

const userSearch = document.getElementById('userSearch');
const userSelect = document.getElementById('user_id');

// Live search as user types
userSearch.addEventListener('input', debounce(function() {
  const q = this.value.trim();
  while (userSelect.options.length > 1) userSelect.remove(1);
  if (q.length < 2) return;
  fetch(`/admin/users/user-search?query=${encodeURIComponent(q)}`)
    .then(r => r.json())
    .then(data => data.forEach(u => {
      const opt = document.createElement('option');
      opt.value = u.user_id;
      const name = u.personData
        ? `${u.personData.name_first || ''} ${u.personData.name_last || ''}`.trim()
        : '';
      opt.textContent = `${u.username}${name ? ' — ' + name : ''}`;
      userSelect.appendChild(opt);
    }));
}, 300));

// Double-click on option = load immediately
userSelect.addEventListener('dblclick', function() {
  if (this.value) document.getElementById('selectForm').submit();
});
</script>

<%- include('../partials/footer') %>
